## Notes

- Model: PVT-PraNet-V1 (PVTv2-B2 backbone).
- Uses the model's LAST output (lateral_map_2) as the final prediction, matching MyTest_med.py.
- The threshold was applied to the RAW sigmoid probability (no per-image min-max normalization), so a frame the model is confident is polyp-free stays empty. max_probability is the raw sigmoid maximum.
- Metrics are computed from the model's own predicted mask vs. the original dataset mask, at the original image resolution.
- Speckle cleanup was OFF: every number below reflects the model's raw, unfiltered prediction.
